This strongly suggests this is **MecA** (also known as MecA/ClpS/ClpX adaptor in Bacillus subtilis). MecA is an adaptor protein that:

2. Also recruits ComK (the master regulator of competence) to ClpCP for degradation

4. Acts purely as an adaptor

- No catalytic protease motifs (ruling out B - protease)
- No DNA-binding domain (ruling out C - repressor)

The question asks about distinguishing its own contribution from interaction partners. MecA&#x27;s own contribution is as an adaptor - it recognizes substrates and presents them to ClpCP. The actual proteolysis is done by ClpP, and the ATP-dependent unfolding is done by ClpC.
